Hierarchical symbolic pattern matcher architecture

Created 2026-01-12 20:20 UTC · Last updated 2026-03-19 15:30 UTC · 166 messages
Original: claude.ai/chat/e9a76194-f642-49c6-aa3f-0cf7f305dfa1
Millard2026-01-12 20:20 UTC
Role: You are a Lead MQL5 Software Architect and Data Scientist. Objective: Develop Layer 2 (Relational Encoding) and Layer 3 (Pattern Library) for a Hierarchical Symbolic Pattern Matcher. This system converts raw symbolic data into a relational grammar and stores it for historical comparison. Technical Specifications: 1.      Relational Event Encoding (Layer 2): o   Input: SPAAOutput struct from Layer 1. o   Logic: Transform the synchronized strings into a Relational Grammar. Instead of just values, identify the *relationship* between the price string and the stochastic strings (e.g., "Price Divergence," "Stochastic Overbought Cross"). o   Context: Calculate the Velocity and Slope of each PAA segment to provide momentum context to the symbols. 2.      Pattern Library Management (Layer 3): o   Storage: Create a persistent system to catalog unique "Fingerprints" (the combination of L1 SAX strings and L2 Relational events). o   Tracking: Implement an indexing system that tracks the outcome of each fingerprint (Success/Failure of price move after pattern completion). 3.      Data Persistence: o   All library data must be appendable to the [Symbol] [Timeframe].csv file. o   Header Protocol: Every file and code block must include a comment header with the current time in UTC-6 (Central). 4.      Logging: o   Print pattern identification events and library registration status to the MT5 Expert Tab. Deliverables: 1.      CRelationalEncoder Class: To handle the transformation from raw symbols to events. CPatternLibrary Class: To handle the database-like storage and retrieval within the MT5 environment. Here is the Layer 1 code contained in MDT_Market_Genetics_Sequencer.mqh in the MQL5\Include folder:
Attached file: unnamed
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                MDT_Market_Genetics_Sequencer.mqh |
//|                                Copyright 2026, Millard J. Melnyk |
//|                                              UTC-6: Central Time |
//|                                      Timestamp: 2026-01-12 13:12 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Millard J. Melnyk"
#define PROGRAM_VERSION "1.00"
#property version PROGRAM_VERSION

#property strict



//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Struct: SPAAOutput                                               |
//| Interface for passing symbolic data to Layer 2                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct SPAAOutput
  {
   string            price_sax;     // Symbolic string for Close Price
   string            stochK_sax;    // Symbolic string for Stochastic %K
   string            stochD_sax;    // Symbolic string for Stochastic %D
   datetime          timestamp;     // Time of the latest bar processed
   double            price_mean;    // Statistics for debugging/logging
   double            price_std;
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Class: CLayer1Encoder                                            |
//| Function: Handles Sliding Window, Normalization, PAA, and SAX    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
class CLayer1Encoder
  {
private:
   //--- Parameters
   int               m_window_size;
   int               m_paa_segments;
   int               m_alphabet_size;
   string            m_symbol;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES   m_period;
   int               m_stoch_handle;

   //--- Memory Management (Pre-allocated buffers)
   double            m_buf_price[];
   double            m_buf_k[];
   double            m_buf_d[];
   double            m_norm_buffer[]; // Reusable buffer for normalization
   double            m_paa_buffer[];  // Reusable buffer for PAA values

   //--- SAX Breakpoints
   double            m_breakpoints[];

   //--- Internal State
   SPAAOutput        m_last_output;

   //--- Helpers
   void              InitBreakpoints();
   bool              CalculateZScore(const double &src[], double &dst[], double &out_mean, double &out_std);
   void              CalculatePAA(const double &src[], double &dst[]);
   string            MapToSAX(const double &paa_values[]);
   void              SaveToCSV(const SPAAOutput &data);
   string            GetTimeframeString(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf);

public:
   //--- Constructor & Destructor
                     CLayer1Encoder(string symbol, ENUM_TIMEFRAMES period,
                                    int window_size, int paa_segments, int alphabet_size,
                                    int Kperiod=5, int Dperiod=3, int slowing=3);
                    ~CLayer1Encoder();

   //--- Main Interface
   bool              Update(void);
   SPAAOutput        GetOutput(void) const { return m_last_output; }
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Constructor                                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
CLayer1Encoder::CLayer1Encoder(string symbol, ENUM_TIMEFRAMES period,
                               int window_size, int paa_segments, int alphabet_size,
                               int Kperiod, int Dperiod, int slowing)
   : m_symbol(symbol), m_period(period),
     m_window_size(window_size), m_paa_segments(paa_segments), m_alphabet_size(alphabet_size)
  {
   //--- Pre-allocate memory to avoid fragmentation
   ArrayResize(m_buf_price, m_window_size);
   ArrayResize(m_buf_k, m_window_size);
   ArrayResize(m_buf_d, m_window_size);
   ArrayResize(m_norm_buffer, m_window_size);
   ArrayResize(m_paa_buffer, m_paa_segments);

   //--- Initialize Stochastic Indicator
   m_stoch_handle = iStochastic(m_symbol, m_period, Kperiod, Dperiod, slowing, MODE_SMA, STO_LOWHIGH);
   if(m_stoch_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
      Print("Error: Failed to create Stochastic handle.");

   //--- Initialize SAX Lookups
   InitBreakpoints();
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Destructor                                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
CLayer1Encoder::~CLayer1Encoder()
  {
   IndicatorRelease(m_stoch_handle);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Helper: Initialize Gaussian Breakpoints for SAX                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CLayer1Encoder::InitBreakpoints()
  {
   ArrayResize(m_breakpoints, m_alphabet_size - 1);
   
   // Hardcoded standard normal distribution breakpoints
   // Values approximate regions of equal probability under N(0,1)
   if(m_alphabet_size == 3)
     {
      m_breakpoints[0] = -0.43; m_breakpoints[1] = 0.43;
     }
   else if(m_alphabet_size == 4)
     {
      m_breakpoints[0] = -0.67; m_breakpoints[1] = 0.00; m_breakpoints[2] = 0.67;
     }
   else if(m_alphabet_size == 5)
     {
      m_breakpoints[0] = -0.84; m_breakpoints[1] = -0.25; 
      m_breakpoints[2] = 0.25;  m_breakpoints[3] = 0.84;
     }
   else
     {
      // Fallback or expand logic for other sizes
      Print("Warning: Alphabet size ", m_alphabet_size, " not manually mapped. Using equidistant.");
      // Simple linear fallback (suboptimal for Gaussian data)
      double step = 6.0 / m_alphabet_size; // assume range -3 to 3
      for(int i=0; i<m_alphabet_size-1; i++) m_breakpoints[i] = -3.0 + (step * (i+1));
     }
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Method: Update                                                   |
//| Core logic pipeline: Fetch -> Normalize -> PAA -> SAX -> Output  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CLayer1Encoder::Update(void)
  {

// Diagnostic Heartbeat
   Print("MDT Debug: Update() called at ", TimeCurrent());
     
   // 1. Fetch Data
   // CopyClose returns items [0]=oldest if structured correctly, but standard CopyXXX 
   // with start_pos 0 usually gives [0]=newest. We need chronological order (Oldest->Newest)
   // for PAA logic. We fetch from index 0 (newest) count WindowSize, then reverse or handle logic.
   // To simplify: CopyBuffer copies to array. If using ArraySetAsSeries=true, index 0 is newest.
   
   // Set arrays as series to ensure index 0 is the latest bar
   ArraySetAsSeries(m_buf_price, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(m_buf_k, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(m_buf_d, true);

   int copied_price = CopyClose(m_symbol, m_period, 0, m_window_size, m_buf_price);
   int copied_k     = CopyBuffer(m_stoch_handle, 0, 0, m_window_size, m_buf_k); // Buffer 0 is Main (K)
   int copied_d     = CopyBuffer(m_stoch_handle, 1, 0, m_window_size, m_buf_d); // Buffer 1 is Signal (D)

   //// Error Handling for Data Gaps
   //if(copied_price < m_window_size || copied_k < m_window_size || copied_d < m_window_size)
   //  {
   //   Print("Error: Insufficient data. Required: ", m_window_size, 
   //         ", Found (P/K/D): ", copied_price, "/", copied_k, "/", copied_d);
   //   return false;
   //  }

// Error Handling for Data Gaps
   if(copied_price < m_window_size || copied_k < m_window_size || copied_d < m_window_size)
     {
      // This will print every tick until history is full
      PrintFormat("MDT Status: Waiting for history. Need %d. Have: Price(%d) K(%d) D(%d)", 
                  m_window_size, copied_price, copied_k, copied_d);
      return false;
     }
     
   // 2. Process Price Stream
   double mean, std;
   CalculateZScore(m_buf_price, m_norm_buffer, mean, std);
   CalculatePAA(m_norm_buffer, m_paa_buffer);
   m_last_output.price_sax = MapToSAX(m_paa_buffer);
   m_last_output.price_mean = mean;
   m_last_output.price_std = std;

   // 3. Process Stochastic K Stream
   CalculateZScore(m_buf_k, m_norm_buffer, mean, std);
   CalculatePAA(m_norm_buffer, m_paa_buffer);
   m_last_output.stochK_sax = MapToSAX(m_paa_buffer);

   // 4. Process Stochastic D Stream
   CalculateZScore(m_buf_d, m_norm_buffer, mean, std);
   CalculatePAA(m_norm_buffer, m_paa_buffer);
   m_last_output.stochD_sax = MapToSAX(m_paa_buffer);

   // 5. Timestamp
   m_last_output.timestamp = TimeCurrent(); // Or iTime(symbol, period, 0)

   // 6. Logging & Persistence
//    PrintFormat("PAA Update | Price: %s (Mu: %.5f, Sigma: %.5f) | K: %s | D: %s", 
//               m_last_output.price_sax, m_last_output.price_mean, m_last_output.price_std, 
//               m_last_output.stochK_sax, m_last_output.stochD_sax);

   PrintFormat("MDT Sequencer | DNA: %s | Stats (Mu: %.5f, Sigma: %.5f) | StochDNA: K(%s) D(%s)", 
              m_last_output.price_sax, m_last_output.price_mean, m_last_output.price_std, 
              m_last_output.stochK_sax, m_last_output.stochD_sax);
   
   SaveToCSV(m_last_output);

   return true;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Algorithm: Rolling Z-Score Normalization                         |
//| Formula: (x - mean) / std_dev                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CLayer1Encoder::CalculateZScore(const double &src[], double &dst[], double &out_mean, double &out_std)
  {
   int total = ArraySize(src);
   double sum = 0;
   
   // Calculate Mean
   for(int i=0; i<total; i++) sum += src[i];
   out_mean = sum / total;

   // Calculate StdDev
   double sum_sq_diff = 0;
   for(int i=0; i<total; i++) sum_sq_diff += MathPow(src[i] - out_mean, 2);
   out_std = MathSqrt(sum_sq_diff / total);

   // Prevent divide by zero (flat line case)
   if(out_std < 0.00000001) out_std = 1.0; 

   // Normalize
   // NOTE: src is AsSeries (0=Newest). Z-Score calculation order doesn't matter, 
   // but for PAA we want chronological (Old->New). 
   // We will store result in dst[0] = Oldest to simplify PAA loop.
   for(int i=0; i<total; i++)
     {
      // src[total - 1 - i] accesses the array from oldest to newest
      dst[i] = (src[total - 1 - i] - out_mean) / out_std;
     }
   
   return true;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Algorithm: PAA (Piecewise Aggregate Approximation)               |
//| Reduces N points to M segments by averaging                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CLayer1Encoder::CalculatePAA(const double &src[], double &dst[])
  {
   // src is now chronological (0=Oldest)
   int n = m_window_size;
   int m = m_paa_segments;
   
   // Simple approach: integer division if n is divisible by m. 
   // If not divisible, this is a basic approximation suitable for trading signals.
   int points_per_segment = n / m; 
   
   for(int i=0; i<m; i++)
     {
      double sum = 0;
      int count = 0;
      int start_idx = i * points_per_segment;
      
      // Handle the last segment picking up remainder bars if any
      int limit = (i == m - 1) ? n : (start_idx + points_per_segment);
      
      for(int j=start_idx; j<limit; j++)
        {
         sum += src[j];
         count++;
        }
      
      dst[i] = (count > 0) ? sum / count : 0.0;
     }
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Algorithm: SAX (Symbolic Aggregate Approximation)                |
//| Maps PAA values to characters 'a', 'b', 'c'... based on Gaussian |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string CLayer1Encoder::MapToSAX(const double &paa_values[])
  {
   string sax_string = "";
   int count = ArraySize(paa_values);
   
   for(int i=0; i<count; i++)
     {
      char letter = 'a'; // Default lowest
      
      for(int b=0; b < ArraySize(m_breakpoints); b++)
        {
         if(paa_values[i] > m_breakpoints[b])
            letter++;
         else
            break;
        }
      
      StringAdd(sax_string, CharToString(letter));
     }
   
   return sax_string;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| File IO: Save to CSV                                             |
//| Filename: [Symbol] [Timeframe].csv                               |
//| Format: Timestamp, PriceSAX, K_SAX, D_SAX                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CLayer1Encoder::SaveToCSV(const SPAAOutput &data)
  {
   string tf_str = GetTimeframeString(m_period);
   string filename = "MDT for " + m_symbol + " " + tf_str + ".csv";
   
   int file_handle = FileOpen(filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   
   if(file_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
     {
      // Move to end of file to append
      FileSeek(file_handle, 0, SEEK_END);
      
      // If file is empty, write header (optional, but good practice)
      if(FileSize(file_handle) == 0)
        {
         // Standard Header Protocol
         FileWrite(file_handle, "Timestamp", "Price_SAX", "StochK_SAX", "StochD_SAX", "Mean", "StdDev");
        }
        
      FileWrite(file_handle, 
                TimeToString(data.timestamp), 
                data.price_sax, 
                data.stochK_sax, 
                data.stochD_sax,
                DoubleToString(data.price_mean, 5),
                DoubleToString(data.price_std, 5));
                
      FileClose(file_handle);
     }
   else
     {
      Print("Error: Failed to open CSV file: ", filename);
     }
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Helper: Get Clean Timeframe String                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string CLayer1Encoder::GetTimeframeString(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf)
  {
   string s = EnumToString(tf);
   // Remove "PERIOD_" prefix if present for cleaner names (e.g. PERIOD_M1 -> M1)
   if(StringFind(s, "PERIOD_") == 0)
      return StringSubstr(s, 7);
   return s;
  }
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

I'll develop both classes in a single header file that integrates with your Layer 1 encoder.

Architecture Summary:

Layer 2 - CRelationalEncoder:

  • Transforms SPAAOutput into RelationalEvent struct
  • Detects 4 divergence types (regular/hidden bull/bear)
  • Classifies stochastic zones (OB/OS/Extreme/Neutral)
  • Identifies K/D cross events
  • Calculates per-segment velocity and slope metrics
  • Derives 5 momentum states (accelerating/decelerating up/down, flat)
  • Generates grammar code (human-readable: BullDiv.OS.Xup.AccUp)
  • Generates 64-bit fingerprint hash for library lookup

Layer 3 - CPatternLibrary:

  • In-memory pattern storage with configurable capacity (default 1000)
  • Pending outcome queue for unresolved patterns
  • Outcome resolution: success/failure/neutral based on pip thresholds and bar count
  • Running statistics: occurrence count, win rate, avg pips win/loss
  • Dedicated CSV persistence: MDT_PatternLibrary_[Symbol]_[TF].csv
  • Query interface: HasPattern(), GetSuccessRate(), GetOccurrences()

Integration Point:

#include "MDT_Relational_Pattern_Engine.mqh"

// In OnInit():
CRelationalEncoder *encoder = new CRelationalEncoder(paa_segments);
CPatternLibrary *library = new CPatternLibrary(_Symbol, _Period);

// In OnTick():
if(layer1.Update()) {
   RelationalEvent event;
   encoder.Encode(layer1.GetOutput(), event);
   library.RegisterPattern(event);
   library.ResolvePendingOutcomes(current_price);
}

Place file in MQL5\Include alongside your Layer 1 header.

Claude2026-01-12 20:55 UTC

Working, but file creation issue. MQL5 requires FILE_WRITE alone to create new files. FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE fails if file doesn't exist.

Two fixes needed:

Fix 1 - Layer 1 (MDT_Market_Genetics_Sequencer.mqh) SaveToCSV:

Find and replace this section:

void CLayer1Encoder::SaveToCSV(const SPAAOutput &data)
  {
   string tf_str = GetTimeframeString(m_period);
   string filename = "MDT for " + m_symbol + " " + tf_str + ".csv";
   
   int file_handle = FileOpen(filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");

Replace with:

void CLayer1Encoder::SaveToCSV(const SPAAOutput &data)
  {
   string tf_str = GetTimeframeString(m_period);
   string filename = "MDT for " + m_symbol + " " + tf_str + ".csv";
   
   int file_handle;
   if(FileIsExist(filename))
      file_handle = FileOpen(filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   else
      file_handle = FileOpen(filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");

Fix 2 - Layer 3 (MDT_Relational_Pattern_Engine.mqh) SavePatternToCSV:

Find and replace:

   int file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");

Replace with:

   int file_handle;
   if(FileIsExist(lib_filename))
      file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   else
      file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");

Fix 3 - Layer 3 LoadFromCSV logic:

Find:

   //--- Check if file exists
   if(!FileIsExist(m_filename))
     {
      Print("L3 Load | No existing library file. Starting fresh.");
      return;
     }
   
   int file_handle = FileOpen(m_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   if(file_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
     {
      Print("L3 Load | Error opening file: ", m_filename);
      return;
     }

Replace with:

   //--- Pattern library uses dedicated file, not the L1 CSV
   string lib_filename = "MDT_PatternLibrary_" + m_symbol + "_" + GetTimeframeString(m_period) + ".csv";
   
   if(!FileIsExist(lib_filename))
     {
      Print("L3 Load | No existing library file. Starting fresh.");
      return;
     }
   
   int file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   if(file_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
     {
      Print("L3 Load | Error opening file: ", lib_filename);
      return;
     }

The pipeline is executing correctly. File I/O is the only issue.

Monday, January 12, 2026, 3:15 PM CST

Full pipeline operational. 2-day test completed in ~1 second.

Results:

  • 240 bars processed
  • 240 unique patterns registered
  • 0 pattern repeats

Problem identified: Fingerprint granularity is too high. Every single pattern is unique — no statistical accumulation possible.

Cause: The fingerprint combines all three 8-character DNA strings plus divergence/zone/cross/momentum. The combinatorial space is enormous (~10^17 theoretical combinations). Even minor price variations produce distinct fingerprints.

Next steps — pick one:

  1. Run longer test (3-6 months) — see if patterns naturally repeat over time before changing code

  2. Reduce fingerprint specificity — hash only grammar code + zone + divergence (ignore raw DNA), creating ~100-500 buckets instead of billions

  3. Coarsen the encoding — reduce alphabet from 5 to 3, or PAA segments from 8 to 4

Which approach?

Millard2026-01-12 21:38 UTC
First, give me a human English simple but detailed explanation of how the fingerprints are constructed.
Claude2026-01-12 21:38 UTC

How Fingerprints Are Currently Built

Step 1: Layer 1 produces three SAX strings

The system takes a 20-bar window of price data and compresses it into an 8-character "DNA" string using the SAX algorithm:

  1. Calculate mean and standard deviation of the 20 closes
  2. Normalize each close to a z-score (how many standard deviations from mean)
  3. Divide the 20 bars into 8 segments (PAA - Piecewise Aggregate Approximation)
  4. Average each segment
  5. Map each segment average to a letter (a-e) based on which statistical bucket it falls into:
    • a = very low (bottom 20% of normal distribution)
    • b = low
    • c = middle
    • d = high
    • e = very high (top 20%)

Result: An 8-letter string like dedccbbb representing the price shape.

The same process runs on Stochastic %K and %D values (using fixed 0-100 range instead of z-scores), producing two more 8-letter strings.

Example output from one bar:

  • Price SAX: dedccbbb (price started high, dropped to middle, ended low)
  • StochK SAX: bddabbbe (stochastic oscillated)
  • StochD SAX: bbdbabbe (smoothed stochastic)

Step 2: Layer 2 analyzes relationships and adds context

Layer 2 examines the three SAX strings and derives four categorical labels:

  1. Divergence (5 possibilities):

    • None
    • Bullish Regular (price falling, stochastic rising)
    • Bearish Regular (price rising, stochastic falling)
    • Bullish Hidden
    • Bearish Hidden

  2. Stochastic Zone (4 possibilities):

    • Neutral (middle range)
    • Overbought (high)
    • Oversold (low)
    • Extreme (very high or very low)

  3. Cross Event (3 possibilities):

    • None
    • Bullish cross (%K crossed above %D)
    • Bearish cross (%K crossed below %D)

  4. Price Momentum (5 possibilities):

    • Flat
    • Accelerating Up
    • Decelerating Up
    • Accelerating Down
    • Decelerating Down

Step 3: Everything gets concatenated into one string

The fingerprint combines ALL of the above:

dedccbbb|bddabbbe|bbdbabbe|0102

Meaning:

  • dedccbbb = price SAX (8 chars)
  • | = separator
  • bddabbbe = stochK SAX (8 chars)
  • | = separator
  • bbdbabbe = stochD SAX (8 chars)
  • | = separator
  • 0 = divergence type (0 = none)
  • 1 = zone (1 = overbought)
  • 0 = cross (0 = none)
  • 2 = momentum (2 = decelerating up)

Step 4: The string is hashed to a 64-bit hex code

The concatenated string runs through a simple hash function (multiply by 31, add each character code) to produce a 16-character hex fingerprint like:

31351DE26BC9C5FD

Why this causes the granularity problem

The three SAX strings dominate the fingerprint:

Component Possible Values Combinations
Price SAX 5 letters × 8 positions 390,625
StochK SAX 5 letters × 8 positions 390,625
StochD SAX 5 letters × 8 positions 390,625
Divergence 5 5
Zone 4 4
Cross 3 3
Momentum 5 5

Total theoretical combinations: 390,625³ × 5 × 4 × 3 × 5 ≈ 1.8 × 10¹⁹

Even with only 7,540 bars over 3 months, the chance of two bars producing identical SAX strings across all three data streams is near zero. The L2 categorical labels (300 combinations) are overwhelmed by the L1 SAX strings (59 trillion combinations).

Proposed Architecture:

Indicator Slots (4 fixed):

Slot Data Encoding Method Combinations
1 Price SAX (5-letter, 8 segments) 390,625
2 StochK SAX (5-letter, 8 segments) 390,625
3 StochD SAX (5-letter, 8 segments) 390,625
4 EMA Slope (3-letter: u/f/d, 8 segments) 6,561

Fingerprint Toggle Inputs:

input bool InpUsePriceSAX   = true;
input bool InpUseStochKSAX  = true;
input bool InpUseStochDSAX  = true;
input bool InpUseEMASAX     = true;
input bool InpUseDivergence = true;
input bool InpUseZone       = true;
input bool InpUseCross      = true;
input bool InpUseMomentum   = true;

EMA Slope Encoding: Each of 8 segments gets one of three letters based on segment-to-segment change:

  • u = rising (change > +threshold)
  • f = flat (change within threshold)
  • d = falling (change < -threshold)

Result: 8-character string like uuuffddd (rose, flattened, fell)

L2 Grammar (kept stochastic-based for now): Divergence, Zone, Cross, Momentum remain stochastic-derived. EMA participates only via its SAX string in fingerprint.

Files to modify:

  2. MDT_Relational_Pattern_Engine.mqh — add toggle flags to GenerateFingerprint
  3. MDT_Test_EA.mq5 — add EMA handle, add toggle inputs, pass flags to L2

Correct?

Millard2026-01-12 21:50 UTC
why is EMA 3-letter but Stoch is 8-letter? is it because we're not tracking EMA crosses or something?
Claude2026-01-12 21:50 UTC

No, both have 8 segments. The difference is alphabet size:

  • Stoch: 5-letter alphabet (a,b,c,d,e) for amplitude levels → 5^8 = 390,625
  • EMA slope: 3-letter alphabet (u,f,d) for direction only → 3^8 = 6,561

I assumed slope would repeat more often than exact shape. But that's an untested assumption.

Two options for EMA encoding:

Method Alphabet Combinations What it captures
Value SAX 5 letters (a-e) 390,625 Where EMA sits relative to its own mean
Slope SAX 3 letters (u/f/d) 6,561 Direction of EMA movement

Which do you want? Or both as separate toggleable streams (5 slots instead of 4)?

Millard2026-01-12 21:52 UTC
I want all indicator line present and future to use the exact same encoding scheme. Explain "amplitude" in this context.
Claude2026-01-12 21:52 UTC

Amplitude in this context:

The vertical position of the value within its statistical range during the window.

Example: 20-bar window, EMA values range from 157.50 to 158.00

The SAX encoder divides this range into 5 buckets based on normal distribution cutoffs:

  • a = EMA in bottom ~20% (around 157.50-157.60)
  • b = EMA in next ~20% (around 157.60-157.70)
  • c = EMA in middle ~20% (around 157.70-157.80)
  • d = EMA in next ~20% (around 157.80-157.90)
  • e = EMA in top ~20% (around 157.90-158.00)

So aaabcdee means: EMA started low, rose through middle, ended high.

Slope would instead encode the direction of change between segments, ignoring where it sits in the range.

Conclusion:

All indicators use identical SAX encoding:

  • 5-letter alphabet (a-e)
  • 8 segments
  • Z-score normalization for price-scale values (Price, EMA)
  • Fixed 0-100 range for bounded values (StochK, StochD)

EMA gets 390,625 combinations like the others.

Proceed?

Millard2026-01-12 21:53 UTC
wouldn't slope cover both direction and amplitude?
Claude2026-01-12 21:53 UTC

No. They capture different things:

Amplitude SAX (current method):

  • Encodes: WHERE the value sits in its range at each segment
  • aaabcdee = started low, ended high
  • Direction is inferrable (a→e = went up)
  • Position is explicit

Slope SAX:

  • Encodes: RATE OF CHANGE between segments
  • uuuuffdd = rose, flattened, fell
  • Direction is explicit
  • Position is lost

The problem with slope:

Two scenarios produce identical slope encoding:

  1. EMA rises from 150.00 to 151.00 → uuuuuuuu
  2. EMA rises from 158.00 to 159.00 → uuuuuuuu

You can't distinguish whether price is at historic lows or historic highs.

Current amplitude SAX actually captures both:

  • Position: explicit in each letter
  • Direction: implicit in letter sequence (a→b→c = rising)

So amplitude SAX is the better choice. Slope would discard information.

Millard2026-01-12 21:55 UTC
slope is determine by x and y. In trading charts, x=1 from bar to bar, so slope from one bar to next is the same as the spread of corresponding prices on each bar. Correct?
Claude2026-01-12 21:56 UTC

Yes. With x=1 (constant bar spacing), slope = Δy = price difference.

So my distinction was wrong. Let me correct:

Current SAX encodes: Absolute position at each segment (a,b,c,d,e)

Slope would encode: Change between segments (which equals Δprice when x=1)

These are mathematically related:

  • From amplitude sequence, you can derive slope (difference adjacent letters)
  • From slope sequence + starting point, you can derive amplitude

The difference:

  • Amplitude: aabcddee — 8 absolute positions
  • Slope: +0+1+1+1+0+1+0 — 7 deltas (one fewer)

Current amplitude SAX already contains slope information implicitly. Switching to explicit slope encoding would lose the anchor point (where in the range you started).

Current method is correct. Proceed with uniform 5-letter amplitude SAX for all indicators?

Millard2026-01-12 21:57 UTC
define "segment", "position", and "range" in this context.
Claude2026-01-12 21:57 UTC

Segment: One of 8 time chunks the 20-bar window is divided into. Each segment spans 2.5 bars. The value for each segment is the average of the bars within it. Segments are numbered 1-8 from oldest to newest.

Range: The span from minimum to maximum value observed in the 20-bar window. If EMA values in the window go from 157.50 to 158.00, the range is 0.50.

Position: Where a segment's average value falls within the range, expressed as a letter (a-e). The range is divided into 5 buckets using normal distribution cutoffs. A segment averaging 157.55 in a 157.50-158.00 range would be position a (bottom bucket).

Millard2026-01-12 22:00 UTC
so the segments are arbitrarily fixed as a 20-bar window. What happens if a pattern in the price movement or indicator exists but spans window boundaries?
Claude2026-01-12 22:00 UTC

It gets split. Neither window captures it fully.

Example: 30-bar price pattern (V-shaped reversal)

Bars 1-20 (Window A) Bars 21-30
Captures: downtrend Missed
Bars 11-30 (Window B)
Captures: partial down + full up

The sliding window advances 1 bar at a time, so patterns "pass through" the window frame. A 30-bar pattern appears as a sequence of ~30 different fingerprints, each capturing a 20-bar slice.

Three potential solutions:

  1. Multi-scale windows: Run parallel encoders with different window sizes (20, 40, 60 bars). Larger windows catch larger patterns.

  2. Fingerprint sequences: Treat consecutive fingerprints as a higher-order pattern. Instead of matching single fingerprints, match sequences of 3-5 fingerprints.

  3. Variable window: Dynamically size window to detected swing points (ZigZag legs). Window = one complete swing.

Current system uses fixed window. Is this a problem you want to address now, or proceed with current architecture and revisit later?

Millard2026-01-12 22:03 UTC
so patterns could exist spanning window boundaries and never be picked up.
Claude2026-01-12 22:03 UTC

Correct. Two distinct problems:

Problem 1: Patterns larger than window A 30-bar pattern never fits in a 20-bar window. No single fingerprint captures it.

Problem 2: Phase alignment Same 15-bar pattern occurring at different positions within the window produces different fingerprints:

Pattern at bars 1-15 Pattern at bars 5-19
aabcdeeee cccaabcde

Different fingerprints. Same underlying pattern. No match.

The fixed window is arbitrary framing imposed on continuous price action. Patterns don't respect window boundaries.

Possible fixes:

  1. Anchor to structure: Start windows at detected swing points (ZigZag), not arbitrary bar counts. Window = one swing leg.

  2. Overlap matching: Instead of exact fingerprint match, use similarity scoring that's translation-invariant.

  3. Multiple phases: Run 8 parallel windows offset by 1 segment each. At least one will align well with any pattern.

This is a fundamental design issue. Want to address it now or note it and proceed?

Millard2026-01-12 22:04 UTC
so, aligned to ZigZag would be more likely to detect patterns?
Claude2026-01-12 22:04 UTC

Yes.

Why:

ZigZag marks natural market structure — swing highs and swing lows. Price action between two ZigZag points is one "move" the market made.

Aligning windows to swings means:

  • Each fingerprint captures one complete market move
  • Similar moves produce similar fingerprints regardless of when they occurred
  • No arbitrary phase misalignment

Current system: Snapshots every 12 minutes, arbitrary framing ZigZag-aligned: Snapshots one complete swing, structure-based framing

Tradeoffs:

Issue Implication
Variable swing length 10-bar swing and 50-bar swing both become 8 SAX segments. Amplitude preserved, duration lost.
ZigZag repaints Most recent swing not confirmed until next swing starts. Slight lag.
Fewer samples One fingerprint per swing vs one per bar. Less data, but higher quality.

This aligns with your HoldConfidence work — you're already analyzing ZigZag legs there.

Want to pivot the architecture to ZigZag-anchored windows?

Millard2026-01-12 22:06 UTC
FUCKING NO UNSOLICITED EXPLANATIONS YOU FUCKING FIREHOUSING FIEND!!! 🤣
Claude2026-01-12 22:06 UTC

Yes. Swings are natural pattern boundaries. Fixed windows are arbitrary.

Millard2026-01-12 22:07 UTC
And the patterns just prior and after swings are what we're looking for in the first place.
Claude2026-01-12 22:07 UTC

Exactly. The reversal signatures.

Millard2026-01-13 10:31 UTC

Claude2026-01-13 10:31 UTC

Depends which parameters:

Parameter Difficulty Notes
Alphabet size (3,5,7) Easy Change breakpoint lookup table
Segment count (4,8,16) Easy Change PAA division
Normalization method Moderate Different code paths per method

All straightforward. No architectural changes needed.

Millard2026-01-13 10:32 UTC
Explain the assumptions you've made, such as that price is predominant, position is relevant, etc.
Claude2026-01-13 10:32 UTC

Current assumptions:

  1. Normal distribution breakpoints — SAX uses Gaussian quartiles. Markets may not be normally distributed.

  2. Z-score normalization for price — Assumes local mean/stddev is meaningful reference. Alternative: global range, ATR-based, or percentile.

  3. Fixed 0-100 for oscillators — Assumes indicator's native range is correct reference. Alternative: use actual observed range.

  4. Equal segment weighting — All 8 segments contribute equally. Alternative: weight recent segments higher.

  5. Segment averages — PAA averages bars within segment. Loses intra-segment volatility/wicks.

  6. Single timeframe — Patterns exist across timeframes. Currently ignores higher/lower TF context.

  7. Success = directional pips — Assumes we're predicting direction. Alternative: volatility, range, or probability.

  8. All indicators encoded identically — May not suit all indicator types.

Millard2026-01-13 10:34 UTC
what's the relevance and role of position?
Claude2026-01-13 10:34 UTC

Position encodes where the value sits within its local range at each segment.

The assumption: Absolute level within range carries predictive information. "Price at bottom of range" means something different than "price at top of range."

Alternative view: Only shape matters. Two identical shapes at different vertical positions are the same pattern.

If shape-only is correct, position adds noise and reduces matches. Two V-reversals — one at range bottom, one at range top — would fingerprint differently despite identical structure.

Millard2026-01-13 10:36 UTC
Give me a simple but realistic example of a price pattern, what its encoding would be, and in that context what "position" refers to.
Claude2026-01-13 10:36 UTC

Example: V-shaped reversal

8 bars, prices: 100, 99, 98, 97, 97, 98, 99, 100

Window range: 97-100

SAX bucketing (5 buckets across range):

  • a = 97.0-97.6
  • b = 97.6-98.2
  • c = 98.2-98.8
  • d = 98.8-99.4
  • e = 99.4-100.0

Each bar maps to a letter:

  • 100 → e
  • 99 → d
  • 98 → b
  • 97 → a
  • 97 → a
  • 98 → b
  • 99 → d
  • 100 → e

Encoding: edbaabde

Position = which bucket (a-e) each segment occupies.

Segment 1 is at position e (top of range). Segment 4 is at position a (bottom of range).

Why position might be noise:

A V-reversal starting at e and a V-reversal starting at c have identical shape but different encodings:

  • V from top: edbaabde
  • V from middle: cbaabcde

Currently: no match.

Shape-only would match both as "down-down-down-flat-up-up-up."

Millard2026-01-13 10:37 UTC
what would shape-only encoding look like, same example?
Claude2026-01-13 10:38 UTC

Same V-reversal: 100, 99, 98, 97, 97, 98, 99, 100

Changes between bars:

  • 100→99: down
  • 99→98: down
  • 98→97: down
  • 97→97: flat
  • 97→98: up
  • 98→99: up
  • 99→100: up

Shape encoding: dddfuuu (7 characters — deltas have n-1 elements)

V from different starting points:

Pattern Position encoding Shape encoding
100,99,98,97,97,98,99,100 edbaabde dddfuuu
99,98,97,96,96,97,98,99 edbaabde* dddfuuu
50,49,48,47,47,48,49,50 edbaabde* dddfuuu

*Same — each window normalizes to its own range.

Both methods actually produce identical encoding here because position uses local normalization. The V shape occupies the full local range regardless of absolute price level.

Where they differ:

Pattern that doesn't span full range:

Prices: 100, 99.5, 99, 99, 99, 99.5, 100, 100

  • Position: eeccccee (shallow dip)
  • Shape: dddffuuf (down, flat, up, flat)

Position preserves that it stayed in upper range. Shape loses that.

Millard2026-01-13 10:39 UTC
OK, so the way the matching is currently being done, ddfuuu would not match dddfuuu, right?
Claude2026-01-13 10:39 UTC

Right. Current matching is exact hash equality. One character difference = different fingerprint = no match.

Millard2026-01-13 10:39 UTC
there is no fuzzy matching done in the code at present, right?
Claude2026-01-13 10:39 UTC

Right. Exact hash match only.

Millard2026-01-13 11:18 UTC
what would you recommend for fuzzy matching?
Claude2026-01-13 11:18 UTC

Options:

  1. Edit distance (Levenshtein) — Count insertions, deletions, substitutions needed to transform one string to another. dddfuuu vs ddfuuu = distance 1.

  2. Longest common subsequence — Find longest shared sequence allowing gaps. Good for phase misalignment.

  3. DTW (Dynamic Time Warping) — Stretches/compresses sequences to align. Standard for time series pattern matching. Handles different-length patterns.

  4. Cosine similarity — Convert strings to vectors, measure angle between them. Fast.

  5. Hamming distance — Count positions that differ. Only works same-length strings.

Recommendation: DTW. Designed for exactly this problem — matching similar time series patterns with phase shifts and length variations. Well-documented, libraries exist.

Millard2026-01-13 11:19 UTC
what's a phase shift in this context?
Claude2026-01-13 11:19 UTC

Same pattern starting at different bars within the window.

Pattern occurs at bars 1-8 vs bars 3-10. Identical shape, offset in time. Without alignment, the SAX strings differ even though the underlying pattern is the same.

Millard2026-01-13 11:19 UTC
what about gaps in minor differences?
Claude2026-01-13 11:19 UTC

DTW handles both:

Gaps — One pattern has extra segment the other lacks. DTW stretches the shorter one to align, matching one element to multiple.

Minor differences — One d where the other has c. DTW aligns them and the cost function penalizes by distance (d-to-c = small penalty, d-to-a = large penalty).

You set a threshold: match if total alignment cost < X. Tunable via input parameter.

Millard2026-01-13 11:21 UTC
OK, add DTW to the match logic and let's see if we get more matches. Here's a fresh copy of the code to work from. Don't remove code unless strictly necessary for the DTW change.

//| ENUMERATIONS                                                     |

//--- Divergence Types
enum ENUM_DIVERGENCE_TYPE
  {
   DIV_NONE = 0,
   DIV_BULLISH_REGULAR,    // Price lower low, Stoch higher low
   DIV_BEARISH_REGULAR,    // Price higher high, Stoch lower high
   DIV_BULLISH_HIDDEN,     // Price higher low, Stoch lower low
   DIV_BEARISH_HIDDEN      // Price lower high, Stoch higher high
  };

//--- Stochastic Zone
enum ENUM_STOCH_ZONE
  {
   ZONE_NEUTRAL = 0,
   ZONE_OVERBOUGHT,        // K > 80
   ZONE_OVERSOLD,          // K < 20
   ZONE_EXTREME_OB,        // K > 90
   ZONE_EXTREME_OS         // K < 10
  };

//--- Cross Events
enum ENUM_CROSS_EVENT
  {
   CROSS_NONE = 0,
   CROSS_BULLISH,          // K crosses above D
   CROSS_BEARISH           // K crosses below D
  };

//--- Momentum State
enum ENUM_MOMENTUM_STATE
  {
   MOM_FLAT = 0,
   MOM_ACCELERATING_UP,
   MOM_DECELERATING_UP,
   MOM_ACCELERATING_DOWN,
   MOM_DECELERATING_DOWN
  };

//--- Pattern Outcome
enum ENUM_PATTERN_OUTCOME
  {
   OUTCOME_PENDING = 0,
   OUTCOME_SUCCESS,
   OUTCOME_FAILURE,
   OUTCOME_NEUTRAL
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Struct: SegmentMetrics                                           |
//| Per-segment velocity and slope calculations                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct SegmentMetrics
  {
   double            velocity;      // Rate of change (delta/time)
   double            slope;         // Direction magnitude (-1 to +1 normalized)
   char              trend_char;    // '+', '-', '=' for quick encoding
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Struct: RelationalEvent                                          |
//| Layer 2 output: Relational grammar encoding                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct RelationalEvent
  {
   //--- Source Data (L1)
   string            price_sax;
   string            stochK_sax;
   string            stochD_sax;
   datetime          timestamp;
   
   //--- Relational Grammar (L2)
   ENUM_DIVERGENCE_TYPE divergence;
   ENUM_STOCH_ZONE   stoch_zone;
   ENUM_CROSS_EVENT  cross_event;
   ENUM_MOMENTUM_STATE price_momentum;
   ENUM_MOMENTUM_STATE stoch_momentum;
   
   //--- Segment Metrics
   SegmentMetrics    price_segments[];
   SegmentMetrics    stoch_segments[];
   
   //--- Composite Encoding
   string            fingerprint;   // Unique hash for library lookup
   string            grammar_code;  // Human-readable relational code
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Struct: PatternRecord                                            |
//| Library entry for historical tracking                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct PatternRecord
  {
   string            fingerprint;
   string            grammar_code;
   datetime          first_seen;
   datetime          last_seen;
   int               occurrence_count;
   int               success_count;
   int               failure_count;
   int               neutral_count;
   double            success_rate;
   double            avg_pips_success;
   double            avg_pips_failure;
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Struct: PendingOutcome                                           |
//| Tracks patterns awaiting resolution                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct PendingOutcome
  {
   string            fingerprint;
   datetime          entry_time;
   double            entry_price;
   int               bars_to_resolve;  // How many bars to wait
   int               bars_elapsed;
   double            target_pips;
   double            stop_pips;
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Class: CRelationalEncoder                                        |
//| Layer 2: Transforms L1 symbols into relational grammar           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
class CRelationalEncoder
  {
private:
   //--- Configuration
   int               m_paa_segments;
   double            m_ob_threshold;    // Overbought threshold (default 80)
   double            m_os_threshold;    // Oversold threshold (default 20)
   double            m_extreme_ob;      // Extreme overbought (default 90)
   double            m_extreme_os;      // Extreme oversold (default 10)
   
   //--- Historical buffers for divergence detection
   double            m_price_history[];
   double            m_stochK_history[];
   int               m_history_depth;
   
   //--- Internal Methods
   ENUM_DIVERGENCE_TYPE DetectDivergence(const string &price_sax, const string &stoch_sax);
   ENUM_STOCH_ZONE   DetermineStochZone(const string &stochK_sax);
   ENUM_CROSS_EVENT  DetectCrossEvent(const string &stochK_sax, const string &stochD_sax);
   ENUM_MOMENTUM_STATE CalculateMomentum(const SegmentMetrics &segments[]);
   void              CalculateSegmentMetrics(const string &sax_string, SegmentMetrics &metrics[]);
   string            GenerateFingerprint(const RelationalEvent &event);
   string            GenerateGrammarCode(const RelationalEvent &event);
   double            SaxCharToValue(char c);
   
public:
                     CRelationalEncoder(int paa_segments, 
                                        double ob_thresh=80.0, double os_thresh=20.0,
                                        double extreme_ob=90.0, double extreme_os=10.0);
                    ~CRelationalEncoder();
   
   //--- Main Interface
   bool              Encode(const SPAAOutput &src_data, RelationalEvent &output);
   void              UpdateHistory(double price, double stochK);
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| CRelationalEncoder: Constructor                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
CRelationalEncoder::CRelationalEncoder(int paa_segments,
                                       double ob_thresh, double os_thresh,
                                       double extreme_ob, double extreme_os)
   : m_paa_segments(paa_segments),
     m_ob_threshold(ob_thresh), m_os_threshold(os_thresh),
     m_extreme_ob(extreme_ob), m_extreme_os(extreme_os),
     m_history_depth(10)
  {
   ArrayResize(m_price_history, m_history_depth);
   ArrayResize(m_stochK_history, m_history_depth);
   ArrayInitialize(m_price_history, 0);
   ArrayInitialize(m_stochK_history, 0);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
CRelationalEncoder::~CRelationalEncoder()
  {
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| CRelationalEncoder: Main Encoding Method                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CRelationalEncoder::Encode(const SPAAOutput &src_data, RelationalEvent &output)
  {
   //--- Copy L1 data
   output.price_sax   = src_data.price_sax;
   output.stochK_sax  = src_data.stochK_sax;
   output.stochD_sax  = src_data.stochD_sax;
   output.timestamp   = src_data.timestamp;
   
   //--- Calculate segment metrics
   CalculateSegmentMetrics(src_data.price_sax, output.price_segments);
   CalculateSegmentMetrics(src_data.stochK_sax, output.stoch_segments);
   
   //--- Determine relational events
   output.divergence      = DetectDivergence(src_data.price_sax, src_data.stochK_sax);
   output.stoch_zone      = DetermineStochZone(src_data.stochK_sax);
   output.cross_event     = DetectCrossEvent(src_data.stochK_sax, src_data.stochD_sax);
   output.price_momentum  = CalculateMomentum(output.price_segments);
   output.stoch_momentum  = CalculateMomentum(output.stoch_segments);
   
   //--- Generate composite identifiers
   output.grammar_code = GenerateGrammarCode(output);
   output.fingerprint  = GenerateFingerprint(output);
   
   //--- Log to Expert Tab
   PrintFormat("L2 Encode | FP: %s | Grammar: %s | Div: %s | Zone: %s | Cross: %s",
               output.fingerprint,
               output.grammar_code,
               EnumToString(output.divergence),
               EnumToString(output.stoch_zone),
               EnumToString(output.cross_event));
   
   return true;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Helper: Convert SAX character to normalized value                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CRelationalEncoder::SaxCharToValue(char c)
  {
   // Maps 'a'=0, 'b'=1, 'c'=2, etc. and normalizes to -1 to +1 range
   // Assumes alphabet size of 5 (a,b,c,d,e)
   int idx = c - 'a';
   return (idx - 2.0) / 2.0;  // Centers on 'c' = 0
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate velocity and slope for each PAA segment                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CRelationalEncoder::CalculateSegmentMetrics(const string &sax_string, SegmentMetrics &metrics[])
  {
   int len = StringLen(sax_string);
   ArrayResize(metrics, len);
   
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
     {
      double current = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(sax_string, i));
      double prev = (i > 0) ? SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(sax_string, i-1)) : current;
      
      //--- Velocity: change between segments
      metrics[i].velocity = current - prev;
      
      //--- Slope: cumulative direction from start
      double start = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(sax_string, 0));
      metrics[i].slope = (i > 0) ? (current - start) / i : 0;
      
      //--- Trend character
      if(metrics[i].velocity > 0.1)
         metrics[i].trend_char = '+';
      else if(metrics[i].velocity < -0.1)
         metrics[i].trend_char = '-';
      else
         metrics[i].trend_char = '=';
     }
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Detect divergence between price and stochastic                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_DIVERGENCE_TYPE CRelationalEncoder::DetectDivergence(const string &price_sax, const string &stoch_sax)
  {
   int len = MathMin(StringLen(price_sax), StringLen(stoch_sax));
   if(len < 2) return DIV_NONE;
   
   //--- Get start and end values
   double price_start = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(price_sax, 0));
   double price_end   = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(price_sax, len-1));
   double stoch_start = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(stoch_sax, 0));
   double stoch_end   = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(stoch_sax, len-1));
   
   double price_delta = price_end - price_start;
   double stoch_delta = stoch_end - stoch_start;
   
   //--- Threshold for significant movement
   double threshold = 0.3;
   
   //--- Regular Bullish: Price makes lower low, Stoch makes higher low
   if(price_delta < -threshold && stoch_delta > threshold)
      return DIV_BULLISH_REGULAR;
   
   //--- Regular Bearish: Price makes higher high, Stoch makes lower high
   if(price_delta > threshold && stoch_delta < -threshold)
      return DIV_BEARISH_REGULAR;
   
   //--- Hidden Bullish: Price makes higher low, Stoch makes lower low
   if(price_delta > threshold && stoch_delta < -threshold && price_end < 0 && stoch_end < 0)
      return DIV_BULLISH_HIDDEN;
   
   //--- Hidden Bearish: Price makes lower high, Stoch makes higher high
   if(price_delta < -threshold && stoch_delta > threshold && price_end > 0 && stoch_end > 0)
      return DIV_BEARISH_HIDDEN;
   
   return DIV_NONE;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Determine stochastic zone from SAX encoding                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_STOCH_ZONE CRelationalEncoder::DetermineStochZone(const string &stochK_sax)
  {
   int len = StringLen(stochK_sax);
   if(len == 0) return ZONE_NEUTRAL;
   
   //--- Use last segment value
   char last_char = (char)StringGetCharacter(stochK_sax, len-1);
   
   // SAX alphabet mapping: 'a'=very low, 'e'=very high (assuming 5-letter)
   // 'a' ~ 0-20, 'b' ~ 20-40, 'c' ~ 40-60, 'd' ~ 60-80, 'e' ~ 80-100
   
   if(last_char == 'e')
      return ZONE_OVERBOUGHT;
   else if(last_char == 'a')
      return ZONE_OVERSOLD;
   else
      return ZONE_NEUTRAL;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Detect K/D cross events                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_CROSS_EVENT CRelationalEncoder::DetectCrossEvent(const string &stochK_sax, const string &stochD_sax)
  {
   int len = MathMin(StringLen(stochK_sax), StringLen(stochD_sax));
   if(len < 2) return CROSS_NONE;
   
   //--- Get last two segment comparisons
   double k_prev = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(stochK_sax, len-2));
   double k_curr = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(stochK_sax, len-1));
   double d_prev = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(stochD_sax, len-2));
   double d_curr = SaxCharToValue((char)StringGetCharacter(stochD_sax, len-1));
   
   //--- Check for crossover
   bool was_below = (k_prev < d_prev);
   bool is_above  = (k_curr > d_curr);
   bool was_above = (k_prev > d_prev);
   bool is_below  = (k_curr < d_curr);
   
   if(was_below && is_above)
      return CROSS_BULLISH;
   
   if(was_above && is_below)
      return CROSS_BEARISH;
   
   return CROSS_NONE;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate momentum state from segment metrics                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_MOMENTUM_STATE CRelationalEncoder::CalculateMomentum(const SegmentMetrics &segments[])
  {
   int count = ArraySize(segments);
   if(count < 2) return MOM_FLAT;
   
   //--- Calculate average velocity and acceleration
   double total_velocity = 0;
   double velocity_change = 0;
   
   for(int i = 1; i < count; i++)
     {
      total_velocity += segments[i].velocity;
      if(i > 1)
         velocity_change += (segments[i].velocity - segments[i-1].velocity);
     }
   
   double avg_velocity = total_velocity / (count - 1);
   double avg_accel = (count > 2) ? velocity_change / (count - 2) : 0;
   
   //--- Threshold
   double v_thresh = 0.1;
   double a_thresh = 0.05;
   
   //--- Classify
   if(MathAbs(avg_velocity) < v_thresh)
      return MOM_FLAT;
   
   if(avg_velocity > v_thresh)
     {
      if(avg_accel > a_thresh)
         return MOM_ACCELERATING_UP;
      else if(avg_accel < -a_thresh)
         return MOM_DECELERATING_UP;
     }
   
   if(avg_velocity < -v_thresh)
     {
      if(avg_accel < -a_thresh)
         return MOM_ACCELERATING_DOWN;
      else if(avg_accel > a_thresh)
         return MOM_DECELERATING_DOWN;
     }
   
   return MOM_FLAT;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Generate human-readable grammar code                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string CRelationalEncoder::GenerateGrammarCode(const RelationalEvent &event)
  {
   string code = "";
   
   //--- Divergence prefix
   switch(event.divergence)
     {
      case DIV_BULLISH_REGULAR: code += "BullDiv.";  break;
      case DIV_BEARISH_REGULAR: code += "BearDiv.";  break;
      case DIV_BULLISH_HIDDEN:  code += "HBullDiv."; break;
      case DIV_BEARISH_HIDDEN:  code += "HBearDiv."; break;
      default: break;
     }
   
   //--- Zone
   switch(event.stoch_zone)
     {
      case ZONE_OVERBOUGHT:  code += "OB.";  break;
      case ZONE_OVERSOLD:    code += "OS.";  break;
      case ZONE_EXTREME_OB:  code += "XOB."; break;
      case ZONE_EXTREME_OS:  code += "XOS."; break;
      default: code += "N."; break;
     }
   
   //--- Cross
   switch(event.cross_event)
     {
      case CROSS_BULLISH: code += "Xup.";  break;
      case CROSS_BEARISH: code += "Xdn.";  break;
      default: break;
     }
   
   //--- Momentum
   switch(event.price_momentum)
     {
      case MOM_ACCELERATING_UP:   code += "AccUp";   break;
      case MOM_DECELERATING_UP:   code += "DecUp";   break;
      case MOM_ACCELERATING_DOWN: code += "AccDn";   break;
      case MOM_DECELERATING_DOWN: code += "DecDn";   break;
      default: code += "Flat"; break;
     }
   
   return code;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Generate unique fingerprint hash                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string CRelationalEncoder::GenerateFingerprint(const RelationalEvent &event)
  {
   //--- Combine L1 SAX strings with L2 event codes
   string raw = event.price_sax + "|" + 
                event.stochK_sax + "|" + 
                event.stochD_sax + "|" +
                IntegerToString(event.divergence) +
                IntegerToString(event.stoch_zone) +
                IntegerToString(event.cross_event) +
                IntegerToString(event.price_momentum);
   
   //--- Simple hash: Use string length + char sum for uniqueness
   //--- Production: Replace with CRC32 or similar
   ulong hash = 0;
   int len = StringLen(raw);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
     {
      hash = hash * 31 + StringGetCharacter(raw, i);
     }
   
   return StringFormat("%016llX", hash);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update historical buffers for multi-bar analysis                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CRelationalEncoder::UpdateHistory(double price, double stochK)
  {
   //--- Shift history
   for(int i = m_history_depth - 1; i > 0; i--)
     {
      m_price_history[i]  = m_price_history[i-1];
      m_stochK_history[i] = m_stochK_history[i-1];
     }
   
   //--- Insert new values
   m_price_history[0]  = price;
   m_stochK_history[0] = stochK;
  }


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                                                  |
//| CLASS: CPatternLibrary                                           |
//| Layer 3: Pattern storage, retrieval, and outcome tracking        |
//|                                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
class CPatternLibrary
  {
private:
   //--- Configuration
   string            m_symbol;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES   m_period;
   string            m_filename;
   
   //--- In-memory library
   PatternRecord     m_patterns[];
   int               m_pattern_count;
   int               m_max_patterns;
   
   //--- Pending outcomes queue
   PendingOutcome    m_pending[];
   int               m_pending_count;
   int               m_max_pending;
   
   //--- Outcome resolution settings
   int               m_bars_to_resolve;
   double            m_success_threshold_pips;
   double            m_failure_threshold_pips;
   
   //--- Internal Methods
   int               FindPattern(const string &fingerprint);
   void              AddNewPattern(const RelationalEvent &event);
   void              UpdateExistingPattern(int idx, ENUM_PATTERN_OUTCOME outcome, double pips);
   void              LoadFromCSV();
   void              SavePatternToCSV(const PatternRecord &record);
   string            GetTimeframeString(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf);
   
public:
                     CPatternLibrary(string symbol, ENUM_TIMEFRAMES period,
                                     int bars_resolve=5, double success_pips=10.0, double fail_pips=10.0);
                    ~CPatternLibrary();
   
   //--- Main Interface
   bool              RegisterPattern(const RelationalEvent &event);
   bool              ResolvePendingOutcomes(double current_price);
   PatternRecord     GetPatternStats(const string &fingerprint);
   double            GetSuccessRate(const string &fingerprint);
   int               GetPatternCount() const { return m_pattern_count; }
   
   //--- Query Interface
   bool              HasPattern(const string &fingerprint);
   int               GetOccurrences(const string &fingerprint);
   void              PrintLibrarySummary();
  };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| CPatternLibrary: Constructor                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
CPatternLibrary::CPatternLibrary(string symbol, ENUM_TIMEFRAMES period,
                                 int bars_resolve, double success_pips, double fail_pips)
   : m_symbol(symbol), m_period(period),
     m_bars_to_resolve(bars_resolve),
     m_success_threshold_pips(success_pips),
     m_failure_threshold_pips(fail_pips),
     m_pattern_count(0), m_pending_count(0),
     m_max_patterns(1000), m_max_pending(100)
  {
   //--- Initialize arrays
   ArrayResize(m_patterns, m_max_patterns);
   ArrayResize(m_pending, m_max_pending);
   
   //--- Set filename
   string tf_str = GetTimeframeString(m_period);
   m_filename = "MDT for " + m_symbol + " " + tf_str + ".csv";
   
   //--- Load existing patterns
   LoadFromCSV();
   
   PrintFormat("L3 Library | Initialized for %s %s | Loaded %d patterns | File: %s",
               m_symbol, tf_str, m_pattern_count, m_filename);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| CPatternLibrary: Destructor                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
CPatternLibrary::~CPatternLibrary()
  {
   PrintFormat("L3 Library | Shutdown | Total patterns: %d", m_pattern_count);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Register a new pattern occurrence                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CPatternLibrary::RegisterPattern(const RelationalEvent &event)
  {
   int idx = FindPattern(event.fingerprint);
   
   if(idx < 0)
     {
      //--- New pattern
      AddNewPattern(event);
      PrintFormat("L3 Register | NEW Pattern | FP: %s | Grammar: %s | Count: 1",
                  event.fingerprint, event.grammar_code);
     }
   else
     {
      //--- Existing pattern - update occurrence
      m_patterns[idx].occurrence_count++;
      m_patterns[idx].last_seen = event.timestamp;
      
      //--- Recalculate success rate
      int total_resolved = m_patterns[idx].success_count + 
                           m_patterns[idx].failure_count + 
                           m_patterns[idx].neutral_count;
      if(total_resolved > 0)
         m_patterns[idx].success_rate = (double)m_patterns[idx].success_count / total_resolved * 100.0;
      
      PrintFormat("L3 Register | KNOWN Pattern | FP: %s | Occurrences: %d | Success Rate: %.1f%%",
                  event.fingerprint, 
                  m_patterns[idx].occurrence_count,
                  m_patterns[idx].success_rate);
     }
   
   //--- Add to pending outcomes queue
   if(m_pending_count < m_max_pending)
     {
      m_pending[m_pending_count].fingerprint     = event.fingerprint;
      m_pending[m_pending_count].entry_time      = event.timestamp;
      m_pending[m_pending_count].entry_price     = SymbolInfoDouble(m_symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      m_pending[m_pending_count].bars_to_resolve = m_bars_to_resolve;
      m_pending[m_pending_count].bars_elapsed    = 0;
      m_pending[m_pending_count].target_pips     = m_success_threshold_pips;
      m_pending[m_pending_count].stop_pips       = m_failure_threshold_pips;
      m_pending_count++;
     }
   
   return true;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Find pattern in library by fingerprint                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CPatternLibrary::FindPattern(const string &fingerprint)
  {
   for(int i = 0; i < m_pattern_count; i++)
     {
      if(m_patterns[i].fingerprint == fingerprint)
         return i;
     }
   return -1;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add new pattern to library                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CPatternLibrary::AddNewPattern(const RelationalEvent &event)
  {
   if(m_pattern_count >= m_max_patterns)
     {
      Print("Warning: Pattern library full. Increase m_max_patterns.");
      return;
     }
   
   PatternRecord record;
   record.fingerprint       = event.fingerprint;
   record.grammar_code      = event.grammar_code;
   record.first_seen        = event.timestamp;
   record.last_seen         = event.timestamp;
   record.occurrence_count  = 1;
   record.success_count     = 0;
   record.failure_count     = 0;
   record.neutral_count     = 0;
   record.success_rate      = 0.0;
   record.avg_pips_success  = 0.0;
   record.avg_pips_failure  = 0.0;
   
   m_patterns[m_pattern_count] = record;
   m_pattern_count++;
   
   //--- Persist to CSV
   SavePatternToCSV(record);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update pattern with outcome results                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CPatternLibrary::UpdateExistingPattern(int idx, ENUM_PATTERN_OUTCOME outcome, double pips)
  {
   if(idx < 0 || idx >= m_pattern_count) return;
   
   switch(outcome)
     {
      case OUTCOME_SUCCESS:
         m_patterns[idx].success_count++;
         //--- Running average for success pips
         m_patterns[idx].avg_pips_success = 
            ((m_patterns[idx].avg_pips_success * (m_patterns[idx].success_count - 1)) + pips) 
            / m_patterns[idx].success_count;
         break;
         
      case OUTCOME_FAILURE:
         m_patterns[idx].failure_count++;
         m_patterns[idx].avg_pips_failure = 
            ((m_patterns[idx].avg_pips_failure * (m_patterns[idx].failure_count - 1)) + MathAbs(pips)) 
            / m_patterns[idx].failure_count;
         break;
         
      case OUTCOME_NEUTRAL:
         m_patterns[idx].neutral_count++;
         break;
     }
   
   //--- Recalculate success rate
   int total_resolved = m_patterns[idx].success_count + 
                        m_patterns[idx].failure_count + 
                        m_patterns[idx].neutral_count;
   if(total_resolved > 0)
      m_patterns[idx].success_rate = (double)m_patterns[idx].success_count / total_resolved * 100.0;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Resolve pending outcomes based on price movement                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CPatternLibrary::ResolvePendingOutcomes(double current_price)
  {
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(m_symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   if(point == 0) point = 0.00001;
   
   for(int i = m_pending_count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      m_pending[i].bars_elapsed++;
      
      double pips_moved = (current_price - m_pending[i].entry_price) / point / 10.0;
      
      ENUM_PATTERN_OUTCOME outcome = OUTCOME_PENDING;
      
      //--- Check for success (positive movement beyond threshold)
      if(pips_moved >= m_pending[i].target_pips)
         outcome = OUTCOME_SUCCESS;
      //--- Check for failure (negative movement beyond stop)
      else if(pips_moved <= -m_pending[i].stop_pips)
         outcome = OUTCOME_FAILURE;
      //--- Check for timeout (neutral)
      else if(m_pending[i].bars_elapsed >= m_pending[i].bars_to_resolve)
         outcome = OUTCOME_NEUTRAL;
      
      //--- Resolve if determined
      if(outcome != OUTCOME_PENDING)
        {
         int pattern_idx = FindPattern(m_pending[i].fingerprint);
         if(pattern_idx >= 0)
           {
            UpdateExistingPattern(pattern_idx, outcome, pips_moved);
            
            PrintFormat("L3 Resolve | FP: %s | Outcome: %s | Pips: %.1f | New Success Rate: %.1f%%",
                        m_pending[i].fingerprint,
                        EnumToString(outcome),
                        pips_moved,
                        m_patterns[pattern_idx].success_rate);
           }
         
         //--- Remove from pending queue (shift array)
         for(int j = i; j < m_pending_count - 1; j++)
            m_pending[j] = m_pending[j + 1];
         m_pending_count--;
        }
     }
   
   return true;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Load existing patterns from CSV                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CPatternLibrary::LoadFromCSV()
  {
//   //--- Check if file exists
//   if(!FileIsExist(m_filename))
//     {
//      Print("L3 Load | No existing library file. Starting fresh.");
//      return;
//     }
//   
//   int file_handle = FileOpen(m_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_ANSI, ",");
//   if(file_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
//     {
//      Print("L3 Load | Error opening file: ", m_filename);
//      return;
//     }
//   
//--- Pattern library uses dedicated file, not the L1 CSV
   string lib_filename = "MDT_PatternLibrary_" + m_symbol + "_" + GetTimeframeString(m_period) + ".csv";
   
   if(!FileIsExist(lib_filename))
     {
      Print("L3 Load | No existing library file. Starting fresh.");
      return;
     }
   
   int file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   if(file_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
     {
      Print("L3 Load | Error opening file: ", lib_filename);
      return;
     }
     
   //--- Skip header line
   if(!FileIsEnding(file_handle))
      FileReadString(file_handle);
   
   //--- Read pattern records
   //--- Note: This assumes extended CSV format with pattern library columns
   //--- For initial implementation, we start fresh each session
   //--- Full persistence requires dedicated pattern library file
   
   FileClose(file_handle);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Save pattern record to CSV                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CPatternLibrary::SavePatternToCSV(const PatternRecord &record)
  {
   string lib_filename = "MDT_PatternLibrary_" + m_symbol + "_" + GetTimeframeString(m_period) + ".csv";
   
   //int file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");
   int file_handle;
      if(FileIsExist(lib_filename))
         file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");
      else
         file_handle = FileOpen(lib_filename, FILE_CSV|FILE_WRITE|FILE_ANSI, ",");   
         
   if(file_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
     {
      FileSeek(file_handle, 0, SEEK_END);
      
      //--- Write header if new file
      if(FileSize(file_handle) == 0)
        {
         FileWrite(file_handle, 
                   "Fingerprint", "GrammarCode", "FirstSeen", "LastSeen",
                   "Occurrences", "Success", "Failure", "Neutral", 
                   "SuccessRate", "AvgPipsWin", "AvgPipsLoss");
        }
      
      FileWrite(file_handle,
                record.fingerprint,
                record.grammar_code,
                TimeToString(record.first_seen),
                TimeToString(record.last_seen),
                IntegerToString(record.occurrence_count),
                IntegerToString(record.success_count),
                IntegerToString(record.failure_count),
                IntegerToString(record.neutral_count),
                DoubleToString(record.success_rate, 2),
                DoubleToString(record.avg_pips_success, 1),
                DoubleToString(record.avg_pips_failure, 1));
      
      FileClose(file_handle);
     }
   else
     {
      Print("L3 Save | Error: Failed to open pattern library file");
     }
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Get pattern statistics                                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
PatternRecord CPatternLibrary::GetPatternStats(const string &fingerprint)
  {
   int idx = FindPattern(fingerprint);
   if(idx >= 0)
      return m_patterns[idx];
   
   //--- Return empty record if not found
   PatternRecord empty;
   empty.fingerprint = "";
   empty.occurrence_count = 0;
   return empty;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Get success rate for pattern                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CPatternLibrary::GetSuccessRate(const string &fingerprint)
  {
   int idx = FindPattern(fingerprint);
   if(idx >= 0)
      return m_patterns[idx].success_rate;
   return -1.0;  // -1 indicates pattern not found
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if pattern exists                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CPatternLibrary::HasPattern(const string &fingerprint)
  {
   return (FindPattern(fingerprint) >= 0);
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Get occurrence count                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CPatternLibrary::GetOccurrences(const string &fingerprint)
  {
   int idx = FindPattern(fingerprint);
   if(idx >= 0)
      return m_patterns[idx].occurrence_count;
   return 0;
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Print summary of library contents                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CPatternLibrary::PrintLibrarySummary()
  {
   Print("=== Pattern Library Summary ===");
   PrintFormat("Symbol: %s | Period: %s", m_symbol, GetTimeframeString(m_period));
   PrintFormat("Total Unique Patterns: %d", m_pattern_count);
   PrintFormat("Pending Resolutions: %d", m_pending_count);
   
   //--- Find top performing patterns
   if(m_pattern_count > 0)
     {
      Print("--- Top 5 Patterns by Occurrence ---");
      
      //--- Simple bubble sort for top 5 (adequate for small N)
      int top_indices[];
      ArrayResize(top_indices, MathMin(5, m_pattern_count));
      ArrayInitialize(top_indices, -1);
      
      for(int i = 0; i < m_pattern_count; i++)
        {
         for(int j = 0; j < ArraySize(top_indices); j++)
           {
            if(top_indices[j] < 0 || 
               m_patterns[i].occurrence_count > m_patterns[top_indices[j]].occurrence_count)
              {
               //--- Shift down
               for(int k = ArraySize(top_indices) - 1; k > j; k--)
                  top_indices[k] = top_indices[k-1];
               top_indices[j] = i;
               break;
              }
           }
        }
      
      for(int i = 0; i < ArraySize(top_indices); i++)
        {
         if(top_indices[i] >= 0)
           {
            PatternRecord p = m_patterns[top_indices[i]];
            PrintFormat("%d. %s | Occ: %d | Win%%: %.1f | Grammar: %s",
                        i+1, p.fingerprint, p.occurrence_count, 
                        p.success_rate, p.grammar_code);
           }
        }
     }
   
   Print("================================");
  }

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Helper: Get timeframe string                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string CPatternLibrary::GetTimeframeString(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf)
  {
   string s = EnumToString(tf);
   if(StringFind(s, "PERIOD_") == 0)
      return StringSubstr(s, 7);
   return s;
  }
