//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                                  MDT_Test_EA.mq5 |
//|                                  Market DNA Tracker - Test EA    |
//|                         Tests L1 Sequencer + L2 Encoder + L3 Lib |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "MDT Project"
#property link      ""
#property version   "1.03"
#property strict

#include <MDT_Market_Genetics_Sequencer.mqh>
#include <MDT_Relational_Pattern_Engine.mqh>

//--- Input parameters
input int    InpWindowSize    = 20;      // L1: Sliding window size
input int    InpPAASegments   = 8;       // L1: PAA segments
input int    InpAlphabetSize  = 5;       // L1: SAX alphabet size (3-7)
input int    InpStochK        = 5;       // Stochastic %K period
input int    InpStochD        = 3;       // Stochastic %D period
input int    InpStochSlowing  = 3;       // Stochastic slowing
input int    InpBarsToResolve = 5;       // L3: Bars to wait for outcome
input double InpSuccessPips   = 10.0;    // L3: Pips for success
input double InpFailurePips   = 10.0;    // L3: Pips for failure
input bool   InpProcessOnBar  = true;    // Process on bar close only

//--- Global objects
CLayer1Encoder     *g_L1Encoder;
CRelationalEncoder *g_L2Encoder;
CPatternLibrary    *g_L3Library;

//--- Bar tracking
datetime g_lastBarTime = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert initialization function                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   Print("==============================================");
   Print("MDT Test EA Initializing...");
   Print("Symbol: ", _Symbol, " | Period: ", EnumToString(_Period));
   Print("==============================================");
   
   //--- Create L1 encoder (8 params: symbol, period, window, paa, alphabet, stochK, stochD, slowing)
   g_L1Encoder = new CLayer1Encoder(_Symbol, _Period, 
                                     InpWindowSize, InpPAASegments, InpAlphabetSize,
                                     InpStochK, InpStochD, InpStochSlowing);
   if(g_L1Encoder == NULL)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create L1 Encoder");
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   //--- Create L2 encoder (5 params: paa_segments, ob_thresh, os_thresh, extreme_ob, extreme_os)
   g_L2Encoder = new CRelationalEncoder(InpPAASegments, 80.0, 20.0, 90.0, 10.0);
   if(g_L2Encoder == NULL)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create L2 Encoder");
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   //--- Create L3 pattern library (5 params: symbol, period, bars_resolve, success_pips, fail_pips)
   g_L3Library = new CPatternLibrary(_Symbol, _Period, 
                                      InpBarsToResolve, InpSuccessPips, InpFailurePips);
   if(g_L3Library == NULL)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create L3 Pattern Library");
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   Print("MDT Test EA Initialized Successfully");
   Print("Configuration: Window=", InpWindowSize, " PAA=", InpPAASegments, " Alphabet=", InpAlphabetSize);
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert deinitialization function                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   Print("==============================================");
   Print("MDT Test EA Shutting Down. Reason: ", reason);
   
   //--- Print library summary
   if(g_L3Library != NULL)
      g_L3Library.PrintLibrarySummary();
   
   //--- Cleanup
   if(g_L1Encoder != NULL)
   {
      delete g_L1Encoder;
      g_L1Encoder = NULL;
   }
   
   if(g_L2Encoder != NULL)
   {
      delete g_L2Encoder;
      g_L2Encoder = NULL;
   }
   
   if(g_L3Library != NULL)
   {
      delete g_L3Library;
      g_L3Library = NULL;
   }
   
   Print("MDT Test EA Cleanup Complete");
   Print("==============================================");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert tick function                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   //--- Check for new bar if processing on bar close
   if(InpProcessOnBar)
   {
      datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, _Period, 0);
      if(currentBarTime == g_lastBarTime)
         return;
      g_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
   }
   
   //--- Process the update
   Update();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Main update function                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void Update()
{
   //--- L1: Update and check if we have valid output
   if(!g_L1Encoder.Update())
      return;
   
   //--- Get L1 output
   SPAAOutput l1_output = g_L1Encoder.GetOutput();
   
   //--- Save L1 data to CSV
   g_L1Encoder.SaveToCSV(l1_output);
   
   //--- L2: Encode relational pattern
   RelationalEvent l2_event;
   if(!g_L2Encoder.Encode(l1_output, l2_event))
      return;
   
   //--- L3: Register pattern
   g_L3Library.RegisterPattern(l2_event);
   
   //--- L3: Check pending outcomes
   double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   g_L3Library.ResolvePendingOutcomes(currentPrice);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Tester function                                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double OnTester()
{
   if(g_L3Library != NULL)
      return (double)g_L3Library.GetPatternCount();
   return 0.0;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Still no matches. 908 unique patterns, all occurrence = 1.

  • DTW Threshold = 3.0 → average distance ≤ 3 per stream
  • Similarity Min = 80% → allows distance ≤ 6.4 total

For 8-character strings, that allows roughly 1-2 character differences total across all 3 streams. Too strict.

Recommendation: Rerun with looser settings:

  • DTW Threshold = 8.0
  • Similarity Min = 60.0

This allows ~2-3 character differences per stream.

Millard2026-01-13 11:52 UTC
what's the input to the DTW code?
Claude2026-01-13 11:53 UTC

Three 8-character SAX strings from the current event compared against stored patterns:

  • price_sax
  • stochK_sax
  • stochD_sax

DTW calculates distance for each stream separately, then averages:

dist_price  = CalculateDTW(event.price_sax, stored.price_sax);
dist_stochK = CalculateDTW(event.stochK_sax, stored.stochK_sax);
dist_stochD = CalculateDTW(event.stochD_sax, stored.stochD_sax);

total_dist = (dist_price + dist_stochK + dist_stochD) / 3.0;

Character distance is ordinal: |'a'-'b'| = 1, |'a'-'e'| = 4.

Millard2026-01-13 11:53 UTC

Claude2026-01-13 11:53 UTC

Close prices. 20-bar sliding window.

Claude2026-01-13 11:54 UTC

Claude2026-01-13 11:57 UTC

Millard2026-01-13 12:00 UTC
1-2: input int InpDepth = 7; // Depth input int InpDeviation = 4; // Deviation input int InpBackstep = 2; // Back Step SetIndexBuffer(0,dbZZ_Line,INDICATOR_DATA); ArrayInitialize(dbZZ_Line,0.0); 3: yes, but not sure if that should be one window or 2 paired windows.
Claude2026-01-13 12:00 UTC

Two options:

One window: Leg before + pivot + leg after → single SAX string capturing the full reversal shape

Two paired windows: Separate SAX strings (before, after) stored together, can match on either or both

The single window captures "the reversal" as one shape. Two windows lets you match approach patterns independently from reaction patterns.

Which do you want? Or both as options via input parameter?

Millard2026-01-13 12:01 UTC
You're not telling me anything I don't know. I'm not sure which would be optimal
Claude2026-01-13 12:01 UTC

Two paired windows. Reason:

You want to recognize an approach pattern as it develops, then predict the reaction. Single window requires the full reversal to complete before matching — too late for trading decisions.

With paired windows:

  • Match approach pattern in real-time
  • Look up historical reactions that followed similar approaches
  • Predict probable outcome before pivot confirms
Millard2026-01-13 12:02 UTC

Claude2026-01-13 12:02 UTC

  2. MDT_Relational_Pattern_Engine.mqh — paired pattern storage, DTW for paired matching, SaveLibraryToCSV() at shutdown

  3. MDT_Test_EA.mq5 — ZZ indicator handle, pivot detection, leg extraction, percentage inputs

Need these inputs in EA:

input int    InpZZDepth         = 7;      // ZigZag Depth
input int    InpZZDeviation     = 4;      // ZigZag Deviation  
input int    InpZZBackstep      = 2;      // ZigZag Backstep
input double InpApproachPct     = 100.0;  // % of leg before pivot
input double InpReactionPct     = 100.0;  // % of leg after pivot

MDT_Market_Genetics_Sequencer.mqh (v2.00)

  • Simplified to pure encoder — accepts arrays, returns SAX strings
  • EncodeArray() — z-score normalized (for price)
  • EncodeBoundedArray() — fixed range (for oscillators 0-100)
  • SPAAOutput now holds paired approach/reaction data

MDT_Relational_Pattern_Engine.mqh (v2.00)

  • PairedPattern struct with approach_sax + reaction_sax
  • DTW matches on approach leg + stoch (not reaction)
  • SaveLibraryToCSV() called in destructor — writes full library with correct occurrence counts
  • LoadFromCSV() reads it back
  • CSV includes PivotType column (HIGH/LOW)

MDT_Test_EA.mq5 (v2.00)

  • ZigZag indicator integration
  • Detects confirmed pivots (needs 3 pivots — middle one is "confirmed")
  • Extracts approach leg (pivot-1 to pivot) and reaction leg (pivot to pivot+1)
  • Applies InpApproachPct and InpReactionPct trimming
  • Encodes both legs to SAX, registers paired pattern

New inputs:

InpZZDepth       = 7
InpZZDeviation   = 4
InpZZBackstep    = 2
InpApproachPct   = 100.0
InpReactionPct   = 100.0

Place mqh files in MQL5\Include\, EA in MQL5\Experts\. Delete old CSV files before running.

double approach_prices[];

  approach_prices[i] = iClose(_Symbol, _Period, approach_start - i);

string reaction_sax = g_L1Encoder.EncodeArray(reaction_prices);
